**[00:02:56.920] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000029)

**[00:03:01.920] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-000030)  
The gentleman of the 1st District of Surigao del Sur,

**[00:03:06.920] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-000031)  
Vice Chair Romeo S. Momo, Sr.

**[00:03:10.920] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-000032)

**[00:03:14.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000033)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:03:17.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000034)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague,

**[00:03:21.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000035)  
the proposed 2027 budget of the Department of Public Works and Highways,

**[00:03:26.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000036)  
is in the amount of 643.95 billion,

**[00:03:32.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000037)  
with the lowest flood control budget since 2019 in the amount of 83.84 billion.

**[00:03:41.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000038)  
A year ago, our nation learned that the people's money meant to hold back floodwaters

**[00:03:48.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000039)  
was stolen through ghost flood control projects as well as substandard.

**[00:03:55.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000040)  
We heard testimony of cash delivered in luggages.

**[00:03:59.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000041)  
Our people waded through the same flood while the funds meant to protect them disappeared.

**[00:04:06.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000042)  
No sponsor of this budget can stand here and pretend otherwise.

**[00:04:13.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000043)  
The question before us is not whether the Department of Public Works and Highways deserves our trust.

**[00:04:20.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000044)  
It is whether this budget is built so that,

**[00:04:24.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000045)  
so that blind trust is no longer needed,

**[00:04:27.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000046)  
so that every peso from each and every Juan and Juana can be checked.

**[00:04:35.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000047)  
This budget is different in four ways.

**[00:04:39.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000048)  
First, accountability.

**[00:04:43.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000049)  
We heard the anger.

**[00:04:45.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000050)  
Alam natin na galit ang tao.

**[00:04:48.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000051)  
After the 2025 Ghost Flood Control Project scandal,

**[00:04:51.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000052)  
our beloved President,

**[00:04:54.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000053)  
Bongbong Marcos,

**[00:04:55.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000054)  
and this Congress demanded reforms.

**[00:04:59.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000055)  
For 2027,

**[00:05:01.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000056)  
the Department of Public Works and Highways scrapped the old parametric formula

**[00:05:08.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000057)  
and the practice where projects could enter the national expenditure program

**[00:05:13.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000058)  
with just a little title and cost.

**[00:05:18.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000059)  
Today,

**[00:05:20.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000060)  
through Secretary Vince Dixon,

**[00:05:22.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000061)  
no projects enters NEP without the four non-negotiable requirements.

**[00:05:29.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000062)  
Number one is the approved program of work.

**[00:05:32.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000063)  
Number two, on-site validated geotag photos.

**[00:05:39.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000064)  
Number four, budget preparation documents such as the BP2O2 form that passes through PDC,

**[00:05:48.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000065)  
or Provincial Development Council,

**[00:05:50.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000066)  
RDC, or the Regional Development Council,

**[00:05:53.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000067)  
supported with resolutions.

**[00:05:57.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000068)  
Signed Certificate of Implementability.

**[00:06:01.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000069)  
Every project will be monitored via DBM Compass

**[00:06:05.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000070)  
and the DPWH Transparency Portal

**[00:06:09.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000071)  
with before, during, and after photos.

**[00:06:14.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000072)  
As of September 23, 2026,

**[00:06:17.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000073)  
the Department has blacklisted

**[00:06:20.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000074)  
nine contractors

**[00:06:22.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000075)  
and is pursuing more than 80 administrative cases.

**[00:06:26.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000076)  
Second,

**[00:06:28.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000077)  
flood management projects return

**[00:06:31.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000078)  
with discipline.

**[00:06:33.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000079)  
DPWH's

**[00:06:35.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000080)  
83.84 billion flood program

**[00:06:38.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000081)  
prioritizes finishing unfinished structures,

**[00:06:42.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000082)  
closing the gaps,

**[00:06:44.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000083)  
repairing pumping stations,

**[00:06:46.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000084)  
drainage and floodgates,

**[00:06:48.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000085)  
guided by river basin

**[00:06:50.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000086)  
and drainage master plans,

**[00:06:52.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000087)  
such as those in Quezon City,

**[00:06:55.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000088)  
Metro Manila,

**[00:06:56.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000089)  
Metro Cebu,

**[00:06:57.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000090)  
Iligan,

**[00:06:58.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000091)  
and other places in the country.

**[00:07:00.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000092)  
It continues foreign assisted projects

**[00:07:03.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000093)  
like the Pasig-Marikina Channel Improvement.

**[00:07:06.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000094)  
Third, equity and resilience.

**[00:07:10.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000095)  
Where the money goes.

**[00:07:12.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000096)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[00:07:15.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000097)  
of the 643.95 billion,

**[00:07:18.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000098)  
P199.58 billion is for OO1, or Organizational Outcome 1, under the capital outlay operation, covering the principal mandates of the Department of Public Works and Highways: construction and maintenance of national roads, bridges, and other related infrastructure.

**[00:07:48.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000099)  
P83.84 billion is for OO2, or Organizational Outcome 2, under capital outlay operation, covering flood control, seawalls, and other related infrastructure.

**[00:08:05.260] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000100)  
P117.74 billion is for Foreign Assisted Projects, covering national roads, bridges, flood mitigation, and national building program.

**[00:08:18.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000101)  
We have per-district breakdown for every district engineering offices.

**[00:08:25.300] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000102)  
Fourth, it begins correcting a decade imbalance.

**[00:08:31.900] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000103)  
For 10 years, Luzon averaged 56% of allocation, but in 2027, Luzon's share falls to 52.62%, and the Visayas and Mindanao together rise to about

**[00:08:48.920] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000104)  
47%.

**[00:08:52.380] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000105)  
In short, the Department of Public Works budget is not to build more, but to build better.

**[00:09:00.460] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000106)  
Build accountable and build resilient budget.

**[00:09:04.820] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000107)  
And on this account, I request the support of this honorable House of the People.

**[00:09:11.420] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000108)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, and this representation will now be honored to answer the questions of our dear colleague.

May time pa, Your Honor, Mr. Speaker. Dahil maigsin ang panahon, mahirap i-comply ang lahat ng requirements.

**[02:44:29.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002787)  
Well, alam naman po natin sa House na meron talagang mga listahan dahil may mga deadline na pinapaabot sa mga opisina na mag-submit kayo ng inyong listahan by a certain time.

**[02:44:45.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002788)  
So, this includes the projects under the...

**[02:44:49.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002789)  
So meron po yan. Ngayon, kaugnay nito, nais kong suportahan yung kaninang panawagan ni Representative Tiangco

**[02:45:01.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002790)  
for 2026. And in fact, extend it as well to the 2025, 2024, and 2023 budget. Dahil po, sinabi ni Secretary Bonoan, siya nagsabi, siya nakakaalam, siya ang sekretary nun, hawak niya ang budget,

**[02:45:21.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002791)  
sinabi niya sa Sandigang Bayan na meron pong allocables ang mga kongresista, baseline 500 million for district, and 130 million per party list representative.

**[02:45:37.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002792)  
Sa kanya nang galing yun. At sworn testimony niya yun, so yan na nga po ang totoo.

**[02:45:44.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002793)  
And this may be confirmed by the independent...

**[02:45:51.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002794)  
independent research of investigative media. So may mga listahan na po, for example, sa PCIJ, na naglabas ng listahan ng mga district allocations.

**[02:46:05.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002795)  
Pero ang problema nga dun, ito yung nire-raise ni Congressman Chanko, e parang lahat ng so-called allocables ay sa district representative lang.

**[02:46:18.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002796)  
Alam naman natin hindi totoo yun. Kasi yun lang ang makikita mo.

**[02:46:21.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002797)  
So, pag tinignan mo yung GAB, yung General Appropriations Act, not the GAB, you can only see the district, diba?

**[02:46:30.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002798)  
Hindi mo makikita, katulad ng sinabi niya, paano yung sa party list? Babagsak din naman sa distrito yan, diba?

**[02:46:37.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002799)  
Paano yung sa senador? Babagsak din sa distrito yan.

**[02:46:41.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002800)  
So, kaya po, tama at sinusuportahan natin ang panawagan na ilabas na.

**[02:46:49.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002801)  
Dahil sinabi na rin ni Secretary Bonone, ilabas na yung listahan ng mga proponents, in other words, from 2023 to 2026, and also for 2027.

**[02:47:03.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002802)  
At dapat hindi malimita sa district representative sa party list, isama din pati mga senador.

**[02:47:13.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002803)  
Alam natin na yung mga nakakasuhan ngayon ng plunder na senators, meron pong,

**[02:47:19.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002804)  
senator and former senator, diba?

**[02:47:22.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002805)  
Na nakasuhan ng plunder, that is on the basis, allegedly, of them being proponents for flood control projects.

**[02:47:31.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002806)  
Pero hindi mo makikita yun sa GAA mismo.

**[02:47:38.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002807)  
Kinailangan pang ma-establish through the testimonies of the district engineers, such as Alcantara et al.

**[02:47:49.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002808)  
Pero, sabi nga, in the spirit of reform and transparency, panahon na po para ilabas na ng buo yung listahan ng mga,

**[02:47:59.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002809)  
and from an official source or official sources, so that would be Congress, Senate, their respective committees on appropriation and finance, as well as the DPWH.

**[02:48:12.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002810)  
Hindi pwedeng sabihin ng DPWH na hindi nila alam dahil si Secretary Bonone mismo ang nagsabi sa senador.

**[02:48:19.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002811)  
Sandigan ba yan?

**[02:48:20.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002812)  
So, ilabas na po ng buo, alin ang proponent ay senador, alin ang proponent ay district representative, at alin yung sa party list.

**[02:48:33.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002813)  
For purposes of accountability.

**[02:48:36.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002814)  
And as I said, dapat, pati sa kasalukuyang budget na tinatalakay natin 2027, moving forward, ganun na rin.

**[02:48:43.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002815)  
Now, is this unconstitutional?

**[02:48:47.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002816)  
Sasabihin ba na, hindi, bawal yan.

**[02:48:49.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002817)  
Bawal yan sa pork barrel decision, PDAF decision.

**[02:48:52.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002818)  
Hindi po bawal.

**[02:48:54.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002819)  
Bakit hindi?

**[02:48:55.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002820)  
Dahil malinaw naman, ang pinagbawal, ang sinabing unconstitutional ng Bellica v. Ochoa is the post-enactment intervention of legislators during budget execution.

**[02:49:08.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002821)  
Diba?

**[02:49:08.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002822)  
Yung PDAF po kasi, ina-approve lang ng lump sum ng Kongreso, pero yung identification ng project nangyayari during the implementation stage.

**[02:49:17.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002823)  
Yun ang sabi ng...

**[02:49:19.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002824)  
ng Supreme Court na, ano na yan, bawal, violation of separation of powers of the co-equal branches.

**[02:49:30.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002825)  
Sa kaso po, nung kasalukuyang sistema, kaya nga tinanggal na yung PDAF system, post-enactment identification, ina-identify na yung project, either during the budget preparation phase, yung RDC process na sinasabi nyo,

**[02:49:48.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002826)  
or during the budget deliberation phase, budget authorization phase, sorry, which is the current process.

**[02:49:58.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002827)  
So, wala pong sabit.

**[02:50:01.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002828)  
Hindi iligal, hindi unconstitutional, and in the spirit of transparency, dapat na talaga na identified na yung proyekto sa proponent.

**[02:50:14.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002829)  
Kung sinong senador, kung sinong congressman, kung sinong...

**[02:50:18.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002830)  
kung sinong party list representative, ma-identify sa project, sasabihin, eh, di EPAL yan, EPAL.

**[02:50:24.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002831)  
Eh, mas mabuti na po na EPAL, kahit pasabihin mong EPAL, eh, at least may transparency and accountability, diba?

**[02:50:34.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002832)  
So, cargo na ngayon, nung, kunyari, party list representative, eh, kung palpak talaga yung proyekto, ikaw ang accountable, hindi yung district representative, diba?

**[02:50:45.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002833)  
Ganon?

**[02:50:46.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002834)  
Okay.

**[02:50:47.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002835)  
So, Mr.

**[02:50:48.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002836)  
Speaker at ginuong sponsor, sasangayon po ba kayo na magpasok na tayo ng special provision sa general, sa budget ng DPWH that will mandate that proponents, including the legislators, senator, congressman, party list, will be identified, you know, through publication.

**[02:51:16.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002837)  
Ah, and this will apply...

**[02:51:18.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002838)  
In the previous budget, for transparency purposes, as well as for the 2027 budget.

**[02:51:24.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002839)  
Sangayon po kayo doon?

**[02:51:25.840] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-002840)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor, hindi na siguro kailangan ngayon.

**[02:51:29.120] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-002841)  
Dahil, ah, sa ngayon, wala hong proponent na congressman or senador.

**[02:51:33.480] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-002842)  
Ang proponent ho ngayon ay ang Department of Public Works and Highways lamang.

**[02:51:37.940] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-002843)  
In fact, sinabi nga nila, bottoms up nga yung kanilang budgeting.

**[02:51:41.740] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-002844)  
That it starts from the DEOs to the regional office or up to the central office.

**[02:51:47.740] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-002845)  
That means...

**[02:51:48.760] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-002846)  
Those who proposes the projects are the Department of Public Works and Highways themselves.

**[02:51:53.940] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-002847)  
Kaya nga, mayroon silang RBPs, yung mga regional budget proposals, that passes through this scrutiny of the Provincial Development Council as well as the RDCs.

**[02:52:04.420] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-002848)  
Yun po ang pagkakaintindi ko sa budget ngayon under the leadership of Secretary Vins.

**[02:52:12.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002849)  
To wind up, Mr. Speaker, alam kong tapos na ang oras.

**[02:52:17.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002850)  
Well...

**[02:52:18.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002851)

**[02:52:22.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002852)

**[02:52:26.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002853)

**[02:52:35.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002854)

**[02:52:41.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002855)

**[02:52:42.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002856)  
Dumadaan lahat sa RDC.

**[02:52:44.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002857)  
No?

**[02:52:44.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002858)

**[02:52:48.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002859)

**[02:52:54.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002861)

**[02:53:02.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002863)

**[02:53:06.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002864)

**[02:53:10.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002865)  
No?

**[02:53:10.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002866)

**[03:23:18.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003208)  
approximately,

**[03:23:19.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003209)  
20 kilometers lang po ito.

**[03:23:21.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003210)  
At sana po,

**[03:23:22.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003211)  
yung mga planners natin isama na rin po ninyo ito.

**[03:23:26.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003212)  
Kasi walang mawawala sa atin

**[03:23:28.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003213)  
kung gagawa tayo ng feasibility study.

**[03:23:30.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003214)  
Kasi kung nandyan na,

**[03:23:31.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003215)  
dadamputin na lang natin yan.

**[03:23:33.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003216)  
Maglalagay tayo ng pundo.

**[03:23:35.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003217)  
Ang Buhol to Cebu Bridge.

**[03:23:40.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003218)  
Mula sa Getafe-Bujol

**[03:23:43.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003219)  
to Cordoba-Cebu.

**[03:23:45.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003220)  
Approximately,

**[03:23:47.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003221)  
18 kilometers lang po ito.

**[03:23:49.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003222)  
Ang Cebu

**[03:23:52.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003223)  
to Santander

**[03:23:54.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003224)  
to Negros Island.

**[03:23:57.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003225)  
Santander-Cebu.

**[03:23:59.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003226)  
To Cebulan-Negros Oriental.

**[03:24:02.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003227)  
Five kilometers lang po ito.

**[03:24:05.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003228)  
Kailangan na natin po ito.

**[03:24:10.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003229)  
Pag na-interconnect natin ang major islands

**[03:24:13.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003230)  
of Luzon,

**[03:24:15.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003231)  
Visayas,

**[03:24:16.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003232)  
and Mindanao,

**[03:24:17.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003233)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[03:24:18.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003234)  
ang mga panaginip ng ating tao

**[03:24:23.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003235)  
ay binibigyan natin ng realisasyon.

**[03:24:26.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003236)  
And,

**[03:24:27.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003237)  
distance will no longer be,

**[03:24:30.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003238)  
will no longer determine

**[03:24:33.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003239)  
the outcome of the progress

**[03:24:36.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003240)  
of each and every major island of our country.

**[03:24:40.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003241)  
Maigiging irrelevant ang distansya

**[03:24:43.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003242)  
at pag nagawa po natin ito,

**[03:24:46.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003243)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[03:24:47.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003244)  
na pwede nating gawin ngayon

**[03:24:50.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003245)  
na may liderato tayo ng DPWH

**[03:24:53.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003246)  
na noon pa ay bilib na ako.

**[03:24:56.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003247)  
At pag nagawa po natin ito,

**[03:24:58.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003248)  
nandyan na ang basic foundations.

**[03:25:01.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003249)  
We will bring our people

**[03:25:04.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003250)  
faster to their destination.

**[03:25:07.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003251)  
We can bring our patients

**[03:25:09.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003252)  
faster to the medical hospitals.

**[03:25:12.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003253)  
And we will bring our families

**[03:25:15.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003254)  
closer together.

**[03:25:16.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003255)  
Pag nagawa po natin ito.

**[03:25:18.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003256)  
Kaya,

**[03:25:19.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003257)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[03:25:20.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003258)  
pwede bang ilagay ito sa susunod

**[03:25:22.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003259)  
yung iba pang major bridges

**[03:25:25.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003260)  
na sinabi nyo nung po naman

**[03:25:27.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003261)  
na gusto ng mga funders

**[03:25:30.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003262)  
lagyan ng pondo

**[03:25:32.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003263)  
ang mga matataas na economic rate of returns

**[03:25:37.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003264)  
na ating major roads and bridges?

**[03:25:41.060] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-003265)  
Tama po.

**[03:25:42.060] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-003266)  
In fact, ang

**[03:25:44.060] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-003267)  
Lady Bohol,

**[03:25:46.060] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-003268)  
Bohol, Cebu,

**[03:25:47.060] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-003269)  
Cebu, Santander

**[03:25:49.060] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-003270)  
ay kasama na sa mga bridges

**[03:25:52.060] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-003271)  
na pinag-aralan nilang isama

**[03:25:54.060] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-003272)  
sa mga priorities nila.

**[03:25:56.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003273)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[03:25:58.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003274)  
alam ko po na pinag-aralan na nila yan.

**[03:26:00.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003275)  
Ang importante po

**[03:26:02.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003276)  
ay malagyan na natin ng bahay sa budget

**[03:26:06.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003277)  
para magsimula na ang tuloy-tuloy

**[03:26:08.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003278)  
na paggawa ng mga tulay na ito.

**[03:26:10.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003279)  
We are an archipelago,

**[03:26:12.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003280)  
but we can connect our islands.

**[03:26:15.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003281)  
Moderno na ang engineering at construction ngayon.

**[03:26:21.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003282)  
Gawin na po natin ito.

**[03:26:23.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003283)  
Hindi lang tayo umaatras.

**[03:26:25.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003284)  
Hindi lang tayo tumitingin sa mga lilit na mga bagay.

**[03:26:28.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003285)  
Gawin natin ang nararapat na kaya naman natin.

**[03:26:32.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003286)  
641.61 billion ang nakalaan sa ating pondo.

**[03:26:37.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003287)  
Kunti lang ito.

**[03:26:39.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003288)  
1.1 billion.

**[03:26:41.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003289)  
Para sa feasibility study,

**[03:26:44.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003290)  
para mabuo itong mga tuloy na ito,

**[03:26:46.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003291)  
mag-connecta,

**[03:26:48.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003292)  
magdikit-dikit ang ating mga tao

**[03:26:51.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003293)  
at umunlad ang ating bansa ng tama.

**[03:26:54.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003294)  
Nasa kamay niyo po yan sa DPWH.

**[03:26:58.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003295)  
Ginigising ko lang po

**[03:27:00.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003296)  
at binibigyan ko kayo

**[03:27:02.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003297)  
ng pagsaludo dahil gusto niyong mangyari ito.

**[03:27:06.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003298)  
At sana sa darating na mga taon,

**[03:27:09.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003299)  
mabigyan natin itong kahulugan.

**[03:27:11.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003300)  
Dahil,

**[03:27:13.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003301)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[03:27:15.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003302)  
habang may bosis pa tayo,

**[03:27:18.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003303)  
tatayo nang tatayo ko ako dito.

**[03:27:21.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003304)  
At magsasangayon naman tayo sa ating good sponsor, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:27:28.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003305)  
Dahil alam ko ang sponsor ay napakagaling din

**[03:27:32.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003306)  
at developmentally minded leader of Surgao del Sur.

**[03:27:39.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003307)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:27:41.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003308)  
Thank you, good sponsor.

**[03:27:44.060] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-003309)  
Thank you, Your Honor. Thank you.

**[03:27:48.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003310)  
And, Mr. Speaker,

**[03:27:51.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003311)  
there being no interpolator

**[03:27:54.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003312)  
on the side of the minority,

**[03:27:56.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003313)  
I move that we terminate

**[03:27:59.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003314)  
the period for interpolation and debate

**[03:28:02.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003315)  
of the budget of the Department of Public Works and Highways.

**[03:28:07.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-003316)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:28:09.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003317)  
Before I continue, may I say

**[03:28:12.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003318)  
the Minority Leader with a vision.

**[03:28:15.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003319)  
Naghang kayong salamat, Minority Leader.

**[03:28:18.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003320)  
Hopefully 2035-37 is in the horizon.

**[03:28:22.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003321)  
What does the House say, Majority Leader?

**[03:28:25.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003322)  
Mr. Speaker, on the part of the majority,

**[03:28:28.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003323)  
we join the minority in the motion to terminate

**[03:28:32.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003324)  
the period of interpolation on the proposed budget

**[03:28:36.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003325)  
of the Department of Public Works and Highways.

**[03:28:39.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003326)  
So move, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:28:42.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003327)  
Thank you, Majority Leader.

**[03:28:44.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003328)  
Naghang salamat.

**[03:28:45.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003329)  
There is a joint motion to terminate

**[03:28:48.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003330)  
the interpolations on the proposed budget

**[03:28:51.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003331)  
of the Department of Public Works and Highways

**[03:28:54.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003332)  
and its agencies and corporations.

**[03:28:57.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003333)  
Is there any objection?

**[03:28:59.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003334)  
The Chair hears none.

**[03:29:01.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003335)  
The interpolations on the proposed budget

**[03:29:04.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003336)  
of the Department of Public Works and Highways

**[03:29:07.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003337)  
and its agencies and corporations is hereby terminated.

**[03:29:16.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003338)  
Congratulations.

**[03:29:22.060] Rep. Patrick Michael D. Vargas** (asr-003339)  
Mr. Speaker, move to suspend session for a few minutes.

**[03:29:25.060] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003340)  
Session suspended.

**[03:32:43.280] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003363)  
Ah, okay natin, ah, go. Session resumed. Majority Leader, please.

**[03:32:52.000] Rep. Patrick Michael D. Vargas** (asr-003363-split-2)  
Mr. Speaker, I move to consider the proposed budget of the Department of Trade and Industry. I move, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:32:59.980] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003364)  
Please proceed, Majority Leader.

Director General Jose Paulo Villaluna,

**[03:34:04.100] Rep. Patrick Michael D. Vargas** (asr-003377)

**[03:34:09.760] Rep. Patrick Michael D. Vargas** (asr-003378)  
Intellectual Property Office of the Philippines Attorney Louie Andrew C. Calvario,

**[03:34:15.900] Rep. Patrick Michael D. Vargas** (asr-003379)  
Aurora Pacific Economic Zone and Freeport Authority President Hill Tawai IV,

National Development Corporation General Manager Robert Samson,

Philippine Economic Zone Authority Deputy Director General Anidel Joy M. Alguzo,

**[03:34:42.800] Rep. Patrick Michael D. Vargas** (asr-003384)  
Philippine International Trading Corporation President and CEO Mary Mercado Tong,

and Philippine Pharma Procurement Incorporated President Kim Lokin.

**[03:34:54.720] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003387)

**[03:34:56.200] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003388)  
We acknowledge, the Chair acknowledges the presence of the heads,

**[03:34:59.980] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003389)  
and officials of the Department of Trade and Industry,

**[03:35:04.900] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003390)  
its attached agencies and corporations.

**[03:35:08.460] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003391)  
On behalf of Speaker Faustino Bojadida III,

**[03:35:12.720] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003392)  
welcome to the House of Representatives.

**[03:35:15.760] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003393)  
Majority Leader.

**[03:35:17.000] Rep. Patrick Michael D. Vargas** (asr-003394)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Vice Chairman of Appropriations,

**[03:35:23.100] Rep. Patrick Michael D. Vargas** (asr-003395)  
Charisse Ann Hernandez,

**[03:35:24.260] Rep. Patrick Michael D. Vargas** (asr-003396)  
of the Committee of Appropriations to sponsor the proposed budget

**[03:35:28.940] Rep. Patrick Michael D. Vargas** (asr-003397)  
of the Department of Trade and Industry,

**[03:35:31.780] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003398)  
To the Vice Chair of the Lone District of Calamba City,

**[03:35:36.400] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003399)  
Charisse Anne C. Hernandez.

**[03:35:38.800] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-003400)  
You are recognized, ma'am. Please proceed.

**[03:35:45.600] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003401)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker,

**[03:35:48.920] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003402)  
and our distinguished colleagues that are still present here.

**[03:35:52.280] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003403)  
I rise to sponsor the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget

**[03:35:56.200] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003404)  
of the Department of Trade and Industry.

**[03:35:58.880] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003405)  
Together,

**[03:35:59.980] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003406)  
with its attached agencies and corporations,

**[03:36:04.300] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003407)  
in the total amount of 10.903 billion pesos.

**[03:36:10.860] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003408)  
In this time of economic uncertainty,

**[03:36:14.860] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003409)  
we are asking the DTI to do more with less.

**[03:36:18.100] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003410)  
So, permit me to state plainly what the more has been.

**[03:36:22.000] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003411)  
In the first half of this year alone,

**[03:36:24.040] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003412)  
the Department delivered 59.5 billion U.S. dollars in exports.

**[03:36:29.980] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003413)  
It brought in 461.85 billion pesos in BOI-approved investments

**[03:36:37.020] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003414)  
and 140.68 billion through PESA.

**[03:36:41.160] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003415)  
Together, 37,000 projected Filipino jobs.

**[03:36:46.820] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003416)  
It has stood up 1,454 negosyo centers.

**[03:36:51.780] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003417)  
And through the SB Corporation,

**[03:36:53.080] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003418)  
it released 5.76 billion pesos in loans

**[03:36:57.940] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003419)  
to 10,170

**[03:36:59.980] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003420)  
MSMEs and conduits in half-year.

**[03:37:05.900] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003421)  
Its shared service facilities have generated 435,982 jobs

**[03:37:13.860] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003422)  
and reached over 841,000 beneficiaries.

**[03:37:17.500] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003423)  
On the trade front,

**[03:37:18.800] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003424)  
three agreements ratified,

**[03:37:20.440] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003425)  
seven signed,

**[03:37:21.540] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003426)  
and two concluded under this administration

**[03:37:24.440] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003427)  
with the ASEAN Digital Economy Framework Agreement

**[03:37:27.940] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003428)  
for signing in November.

**[03:37:29.980] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003429)  
The Philippines-Chile CEPA concluded

**[03:37:33.380] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003430)  
and the Philippines-European Union FTA within reach.

**[03:37:39.560] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003431)  
And on consumer protection,

**[03:37:41.220] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003432)  
14,424 firms monitored,

**[03:37:45.400] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003433)  
5,025 establishments checked for price and supply,

**[03:37:49.680] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003434)  
and 512 Philippine national standards promulgated.

**[03:37:53.660] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003435)  
Mr. Speaker, these are not aspirations.

**[03:37:57.660] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003436)  
These are receipt.

**[03:37:59.980] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003437)  
Mr. Speaker, the common thread is this.

**[03:38:02.780] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003438)  
Ang bawat pisong pupondohan natin

**[03:38:04.800] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003439)  
ay magiging bahagi po ng kwento ng isang Pilipino,

**[03:38:08.780] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003440)  
ang isang export business

**[03:38:10.140] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003441)  
na sinasara niya ang kanyang unang international order,

**[03:38:14.260] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003442)  
ang isang sari-sari store owner

**[03:38:15.940] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003443)  
na magkakaroon ng puhunan sa negosyo

**[03:38:18.920] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003444)  
at ang karaniwang Pilipino na namamalingke

**[03:38:22.020] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003445)  
na makakasiguradong ligtas at bago

**[03:38:25.540] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003446)  
ang nabibili niyang mga produkto.

**[03:38:27.940] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003447)  
At ito ay mura din,

**[03:38:29.100] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003448)  
at ito ay abot.

**[03:38:29.980] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003449)  
Kaya.

**[03:38:30.940] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003450)  
Additional accomplishments may be mentioned,

**[03:38:33.120] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003451)  
but I'm looking forward to mention it later.

**[03:38:38.460] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003452)  
So we are welcoming these questions

**[03:38:41.760] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003453)  
and these discussions on the floor.

**[03:38:43.740] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003454)  
But may I just add, Mr. Speaker,

**[03:38:45.140] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003455)  
that a properly funded DTI has the power

**[03:38:48.400] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003456)  
to turn simple dreams into realities

**[03:38:51.860] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003457)  
for ordinary Filipinos.

**[03:38:53.760] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003458)  
So we have the power to convert the people's industry

**[03:38:57.480] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003459)  
into their prosperity.

**[03:38:58.880] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003460)  
I therefore respectfully urge my colleagues

**[03:39:01.760] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003461)  
to consider the restoration and augmentation

**[03:39:04.520] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003462)  
na mamayarin po i-request natin.

**[03:39:07.380] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003463)  
Again, Mr. Speaker, thank you.

**[03:39:08.640] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-003464)  
And I open the floor to questions.

**[04:13:07.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004292)  
So, ang mga katanungan po, of course,

**[04:13:09.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004293)

So, kung mag-chronology lang po tayo muna sa Pax Silica, tama po ba ito?

**[04:13:30.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004295)  
So, December 12, 2025, unang ni-launch sa Washington, D.C.,

**[04:13:39.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004296)  
yung Pax Silica Initiative.

**[04:13:41.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004297)

**[04:13:46.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004298)

**[04:13:54.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004299)

**[04:14:06.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004300)

**[04:14:09.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004301)

**[04:14:39.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004306)

**[05:03:48.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005596)

**[05:03:58.180] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005601)  
Kaya, ganun po ka, ano ang

**[05:04:00.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005602)  
pangangailangan natin na

**[05:04:02.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005603)

**[05:04:04.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005604)

**[05:04:08.760] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005606)

**[05:04:14.580] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005608)

**[05:04:15.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005609)  
the distinguished sponsor

**[05:04:18.400] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005610)  
regarding the budget of

**[05:04:20.760] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005611)

**[05:04:22.140] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005612)  
Yes.

**[05:04:23.940] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005613)  
How many CDA regionalized

**[05:04:26.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005614)  
offices presently, no?

**[05:04:29.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005615)  
Na mayroon silang

**[05:04:31.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005616)  
mga permanent buildings?

**[05:04:33.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005617)  
At ilan po yung

**[05:04:35.580] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005618)  
still operating from

**[05:04:37.220] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005619)

**[05:04:39.700] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005620)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[05:04:41.080] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005621)

**[05:04:42.700] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005622)

**[05:04:45.160] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005623)

**[05:04:46.880] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005624)

**[05:04:48.140] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005625)  
Okay.

**[05:04:58.960] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005626)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleagues, currently po meron pong walo. Pasensya na po, Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleagues.

**[05:05:25.360] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005627)  
Thank you po sa pasensya.

**[05:05:27.060] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005628)  
Ang ngayon po, currently meron pong 13 na mga regional offices ang CDA.

**[05:05:34.080] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005629)  
Ang walo po dito ay leased facilities pero ang apat po for repair or for rehabilitation.

**[05:05:41.640] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005630)  
Ang tatlo po, ang katunayan po nito ay for demolition and construction na po ng bagong opisina.

**[05:05:48.260] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005631)  
At may tatlo po sa CAR, sa 11 at sa Caraga na may proposal na mag-construct ng bagong CDA training center.

**[05:05:56.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005632)  
Thank you, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[05:06:00.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005633)  
Napansin ko sa 2027, parang zero yung capital outlay.

**[05:06:08.860] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005634)  
So, paano po yan?

**[05:06:12.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005635)  
Paano po natin magsara yung mga opisina na ito?

**[05:06:18.320] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005636)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleagues, dapat po hindi.

**[05:06:22.980] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005637)  
At we are asking again.

**[05:06:26.480] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005638)  
May request po at proposal na ma-augment pa po at magkaroon po ng budget po para dito for these buildings.

**[05:06:37.720] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005639)  
At dahil po we know the urgency and the need of making sure na may building po per region.

**[05:06:50.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005640)  
Yan po bang CDA na attached agency ng DTI?

**[05:06:56.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005641)  
Sa tingin nyo ba, gusto nyo pang maka-attach sila sa inyo o ayaw na ninyo din sa kanila?

**[05:07:06.180] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005642)  
Katulad nung sinabi nyo kanina na hindi naman mandato ninyo yung ganon.

**[05:07:13.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005643)  
So, may I ask, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, baka gusto rin ninyo na ipalipat sila sa ibang agency?

**[05:07:22.400] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005644)  
Kasi zero na nga yung ano nila eh.

**[05:07:24.780] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005645)  
Si Nero na nga eh.

**[05:07:26.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005646)  
Tapos ngayon, napapansin ko sa MOOE, no?

**[05:07:32.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005647)  
Magkano ba yung 2027?

**[05:07:38.220] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005648)  
Negative pa nga eh. Negative 45.4% yung MOOE.

**[05:07:46.660] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005649)  
So, parang indirectly, you are telling CDA na,

**[05:07:56.180] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005650)  
umalis na nga kayo sa tahanan namin?

**[05:07:58.940] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005651)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleagues, may I respond?

**[05:08:02.300] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005652)  
Hindi po.

**[05:08:04.740] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005653)  
Nung kasama po ang buong proposal,

**[05:08:08.480] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005654)  
hindi po ang ahensya ng DTI ang nang-zero dito.

**[05:08:16.500] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005655)  
Meron pong kaakibat na pondo po, mayroong nirequest po para dito,

**[05:08:22.520] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005656)  
pero hindi po DTI ang naglagay ng zero.

**[05:08:25.460] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005657)  
Kandi, Distinguished Sponsor, Mr. Speaker,

**[05:08:29.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005658)  
labi, magkaibigan naman kayo ng chairperson sa APRO.

**[05:08:35.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005659)  
Kaya nga, ikaw yung pinili na mag-sponsor sa budget ng DTI kasama si CDA.

**[05:08:42.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005660)  
Kandi, Distinguished Sponsor, commit na may labi man lang kahit may maidagdag na 1 piso dito sa CDA.

**[05:08:49.900] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005661)  
Bahala na kayo sa 1 piso.

**[05:08:51.340] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005662)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleagues, ang inyo pong lingkod,

**[05:08:55.080] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005663)  
will definitely lobby.

**[05:08:57.260] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005664)  
And ito po, specifically, coming from the data or sa request po nabigay po ng CDA,

**[05:09:04.940] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005665)  
there's actually seeking, just for the record, an additional of 846 million

**[05:09:10.560] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005666)  
para po sa enterprise development, market enhancement, regional office buildings in 7 regions.

**[05:09:18.320] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005667)  
At talaga pong kasama po ang CDA at DTI, collective effort talaga para po magkaroon ng

**[05:09:24.900] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005668)  
mas inclusive development sa lahat, sa buong bansa po.

**[05:09:29.700] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005669)  
And definitely, we will fight for the budget of CDA na mapondohan po ito.

**[05:09:36.780] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005670)  
Kaya maraming salamat din po sa pagtayo po dito at sa pag-raise po ng concern na ito, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleagues.

**[05:09:45.860] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005671)  
Distinguished Sponsor, taga-electric cooperative po ako.

**[05:09:49.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005672)  
Pareho po, cooperative ang apelido namin. Magkapatid po kami.

**[05:09:53.740] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005673)  
So...

**[05:09:54.340] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005674)  
Yes po.

**[05:09:56.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005675)  
Lahat ng team, mga kamag-anak po namin yan.

**[05:10:03.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005676)  
Finally, dito sa CDA issue, papano po yung ating RISE, yung Recovery Innovation, Sustainability and Empowerment program natin,

**[05:10:14.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005677)  
mayroon po bang pondo para dito?

**[05:10:19.480] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005678)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleagues, dun po sa cooperative,

**[05:10:24.340] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005679)  
RISE,

**[05:10:26.340] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005680)  
we are also requesting, kasama na po ito, diba po?

**[05:10:31.540] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005681)  
Kasama na po para po sa recovery, innovation, sustainability and empowerment na magkaroon o mabalik o magkapondo po ng 120 million,

**[05:10:47.760] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005682)  
that this fund will make sure, that this fund kasi po will definitely,

**[05:10:54.340] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005683)  
may benefit cooperatives, like yung sinabi po ninyo na kooperatiba.

**[05:11:00.720] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005684)  
May proposed request po tayo na 120 million para po sa RISE, cooperative RISE.

**[05:11:09.860] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005685)  
Thank you, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[05:11:11.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005686)  
Finally, on the issue of CDA, mga pangakailangan ng CDA, this representation would like to manifest that I,

**[05:11:24.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005687)  
personally, I am personally supporting the additional funding of CDA in terms of the construction of your regional offices,

**[05:11:38.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005688)  
cooperative development, cooperative enterprise development, yung mga market enhancement program natin,

**[05:11:48.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005689)  
more or less 150 million din yung sa...

**[05:11:54.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005690)  
RISE na 120 million, nandito naman siguro yung taga DBM dahil normally naman may representative dito sa DBM,

**[05:12:03.840] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005691)  
nagtatago nga lang dahil sila ang kumakalta sa mga budget.

**[05:12:08.760] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005692)  
Sana narinig ninyo kung nasa CR kayo o saan, taga DBM, may pangangailangan tayo.

**[05:12:15.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005693)  
So, now to another issue, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[05:12:22.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005694)  
Nakikita ko, dito sa...

**[05:12:24.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005695)  
budget utilization ratio ng DTI, it is declining, no?

**[05:12:32.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005696)  
You have a declining overall budget utilization sa 20...

**[05:12:39.260] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005697)  
from 91.3% sa 2023, nagiging 84.2% sa 2025.

**[05:12:46.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005698)  
Then, pababa pa siya ngayon at...

**[05:12:54.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005699)  
pababa pa siya ng 71% sa 2025.

**[05:12:58.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005700)  
Mid-year 2026, approximately 5.8 billion of released allotments remain unobligated.

**[05:13:07.760] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005701)  
So, I believe that the issue is not merely whether appropriations were released.

**[05:13:13.540] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005702)  
There appears to be a widening gap between the budget, authority, obligation, actual payment, and delivery of program.

**[05:13:23.220] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005703)  
So,

**[05:13:24.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005704)  
papano po nangyari itong may ginagawa na ba tayo to resolve this issue?

**[05:13:30.880] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005705)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[05:13:32.720] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005706)  
Distinguished Colleagues,

**[05:13:36.580] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005707)  
maihabol ko lang po, Mr. Speaker, na yung budget nga po natin ngayon for 2027 is 11% smaller.

**[05:13:43.320] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005708)  
Mahabol ko lang po yun, kaya siguro isa po yun sa naapektuhan po ang cooperatives now with the utilization.

**[05:13:52.140] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005709)  
And just to settle din po yung issue about the utilization ng funding.

**[05:14:01.500] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005710)  
Wait.

**[05:14:02.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005711)  
Ang hindi ko maintindihan, Mr. Speaker, parang DTI budget talaga to may problema sa utilization.

**[05:14:19.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005712)  
Bakit si CDE ang nabawasan?

**[05:14:22.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005713)  
Bakit kung si Juan ang may problema, si Pedro ang kinastigo?

**[05:14:29.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005714)  
Yan po yung hindi ko maintindihan.

**[05:14:31.220] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005715)  
Tapos, napapansin ko po na yung pinakamalaking bahagi ng inyong budget ay concentrated doon sa Office of the Secretary.

**[05:14:44.460] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005716)  
Bakit po parang 76% yata ay nakalagak sa Office of the Secretary?

**[05:14:54.820] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005719)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, hindi po.

**[05:15:00.000] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005720)  
Ah, okay. Yung budget po na sinasabi sa Office of the Secretary ay hindi po ito, as a whole of DTI po ito, Mr. Speaker, distinguished speaker.

**[05:15:15.840] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005721)  
So, naman po sa question or concern about the utilization, nagkaroon po ng delay sa fund utilization at masabi po natin dahil din sa late approval ng mga bidding, ng mga documentary requirements po.

**[05:15:34.580] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005722)  
At may mga so on and so forth. We can also give it to you specifically. We have the data here po, Mr. Speaker. May mga delays po.

**[05:15:44.820] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005723)  
Of course, muli.

**[05:15:45.840] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005724)  
Mula po sa ahensya kung saan nag-approve po natin ito.

**[05:15:49.780] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005725)  
Anyway, distinguished sponsor, salamat doon sa kasagutan. Ang huli ko na lang hiling sa DTI, isubmit nyo na yung hiningi ko na dita regarding doon sa cost of products, yung percentage cost of electricity in our

**[05:16:15.840] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005726)  
cost of production. So, ang ibig kong sabihin, halimbawa, yung isang produkto na ito, magkano ba yung percentage cost ng electricity? Matagal ko na pong hiningi yan. Wala pa pong nagbigay kasi kailangan namin yan sa Committee on Energy para matantya natin gaano kalaki ang epekto ng pagtaas ng cost ng korinti.

**[05:16:45.840] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005727)  
Doon sa cost of products and services. So, kahit, ano, ibigay nyo bago magtapos yung termino ko sa 2028, basta ibigay nyo. Anyway, magamit pa naman yan sa susunod. Pero, kung talagang nagtatrabaho kayo sa trabaho ninyo, mayroon kayo yan. Can you commit, distinguished sponsor?

**[05:17:06.740] Rep. Charisse Anne C. Hernandez** (asr-005728)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished release, yes, we can commit and we will submit. And rest assured that the DTI is doing the best it can.

**[05:17:13.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005729)

**[05:26:36.960] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005803)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleagues,

**[05:26:39.480] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005804)  
when this Chamber last considered

**[05:26:41.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005805)  
the proposed fiscal year 2027

**[05:26:43.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005806)  
budget of the DICT and its attached agencies, important concerns were raised on online safety, platform accountability, digital government connectivity, and the delivery of digital services.

**[05:27:00.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005807)  
Since then, the DICT and its attached agencies have provided updates and taken concrete steps in response.

**[05:27:08.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005808)  
On online safety and platform accountability, DICT continues to engage with META and other platforms.

**[05:27:17.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005809)  
A show-cause order has been issued to META USA while the NPC has raised concerns on platform safeguards and AI-related or generated content.

**[05:27:31.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005810)  
CICC led discussions with Reddit, Discord, and Roblox,

**[05:27:37.120] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005811)  
and have also led to

**[05:27:38.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005812)  
commitments to establish Philippine presence, strengthening government accountability and legal jurisdiction for the platforms.

**[05:27:48.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005813)  
Secretary Henry Aguda has assured me that DICT is actively studying international best practices

**[05:27:55.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005814)  
and has requested from META a report on its coordination and accomplishments for 2025 to 2026.

**[05:28:05.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005815)  
Further, the NBI and CICC

**[05:28:08.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005816)  
intervened with a 15-year-old student in General Mariano Alvarez, Cavite, yesterday

**[05:28:15.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005817)  
who was allegedly linked to a network discussing mass school shootings

**[05:28:20.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005818)  
and found on him a playing card that read,

**[05:28:25.740] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005819)  
commit suicide and commit mass shooting.

**[05:28:29.500] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005820)  
This resulted in the voluntary surrender of his phone for forensic examination

**[05:28:35.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005821)  
and his referral to the MSWDO,

**[05:28:38.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005822)  
for monitoring and psychological assessment.

**[05:28:41.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005823)  
These remain ongoing matters,

**[05:28:43.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005824)  
but the objective is clear.

**[05:28:45.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005825)  
Mechanisms that are lawful, workable, and responsive to the concerns of the Filipino.

**[05:28:51.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005826)  
And to be clear, and for the record,

**[05:28:55.500] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005827)  
the House did not call on META or Mark Zuckerberg to appear before this chamber.

**[05:29:01.300] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-005828)
